# Phrase index

Standing rule: **one construction per printed phrase**, pool-wide. Two constructions for one phrase are two behaviours for one card text.

Generated by `python3 tools/phrase_index.py --write`. Do not edit by hand.

## Normalization

Each authored node that carries `printed` (ability, mode, clause root) is one row.
The phrase is normalized so rows compare across cards, in this order:

1. Markup stripping — same walk as coverage (`<hr>` → newline; drop `<b>`/`<i>`/`<color>`/`<ridx>` and leftover tags).
2. Strip the leading trigger prefix already encoded by `on` / mode `kind`: `Fanfare:`, `Last Words:`, `Evolve:`, `Super-Evolve:`, `Strike:`, `Follower Strike:`, `Clash:`, `Enhance (N):`, `Combo (N):`, `Rally (N):`, `Engage:`, `Spellboost:`, `Countdown (N)`. `At the start/end of your turn,` and `Skybound Art` / `Super Skybound Art` stay — they are the phrase (a condition, not `on`).
3. `Crest: <catalog name>` → `CREST`; remaining catalog names (cards, tokens, crests; longest first) → `NAME`.
4. `+N/+N` stat pairs stay `+N/+N`; other digits → `N`. `X is …` keeps `X`.
5. Trailing period stripped; whitespace collapsed.

The construction shape is the node's JSON with every `printed` removed, numeric literals → `N`, card/crest ids → `ID`, binding names (`as`, `ref`) → `REF`, then canonical JSON (sorted keys). Ability `on`/`whose` and mode `kind`/`cost` are omitted from the shape — they are the stripped prefix. Two nodes share a construction when the shapes are byte-equal. Divergence is judged per node kind (ability / mode / clause) so a Fanfare wrapper is not compared to its inner clause.

Index: **2054** rows, **869** distinct phrases, **856** with one shape (per kind), **13** divergent (16 kind×phrase groups).

## Exceptions

From `tools/phrase-index-exceptions.json`. Each entry names ids and the sentence that forces the difference.

| phrase | reason | ids |
|---|---|---|
| Add a NAME to your hand | Leafshadow Assassin 10912110 binds the Fairy so Combo (3) can give that instance Bane; a lone add has no later clause to bind. | `10912110`, `10011210` |
| Deal N damage to all enemy followers | Timepiece of Perfection 10762210 wraps the Enhance copy in seq so validate.mjs does not flag a mode that copies the Engage clause's damage subtree. | `10762210`, `10422110` |
| Deal N damage instead | An instead-clause inherits its target from the card's base sentence (all enemies / all enemy followers / the selected follower), which the leftover printed does not repeat. | `10512310`, `10923310`, `90024320` |
| Draw N cards | Ebb and Flow 10853310 binds the drawn cards so the super-evolution clause can reduce their costs; a lone draw has nothing to bind. | `10853310`, `10052310` |
| Draw a follower | Blade of the Crestpetal 10541310 binds the drawn follower so X is that card's cost; Adventurers' Guild 10002210 draws with no later reference. | `10541310`, `10002210` |
| Select a Mode to activate | The numbered options are card-specific and carry their own printed nodes; this prompt is only the choose wrapper. | `10864120`, `10823310`, `10962310`, `10912310`, `10403110`, `10771310`, `10413310`, `10743110` |
| Select an enemy follower on the field and deal it N damage | Measured Attunement 10721310 binds the damaged follower so Rally (10) can give that instance Can't attack until the end of the opponent's turn. | `10721310`, `10041110` |
| Summon N copies of NAME | Amorous Necromancer / Advent of the Eld Sword / Ferocious Commander bind the summoned copies so a later Super-Evolve or additive Enhance can give them Drain or stats; a lone summon has no later clause. | `10052120`, `10621310`, `10922120`, `10513110` |
| Summon a NAME | Zeta & Bea 10424110 and Mireille & Risette 10432120 bind the summoned copy so Enhance / Earth Rite can give it Bane or evolve it; a lone summon has no later clause. | `10424110`, `10432120`, `10031320` |
| Summon an NAME and NAME | Marionette Master 10571110 binds both puppets so Enhance (7) can give them Storm; Blade Puppeteer 10972120 has no later clause on those instances. | `10571110`, `10972120` |
| Whenever a NAME is destroyed, restore N defense to your leader | Lifestealer 10553110 prints "a Skeleton" with no side, so both ally_follower_destroyed and enemy_follower_destroyed are required (docs/schema.md Events). | `10553110` |
| Whenever an allied Marine follower attacks, give it +N/+N until the end of the turn and, once on each of your turns, add a NAME to your hand | Yube's crest is one printed sentence with two triggers (always-buff vs once-per-your-turn add), so the same phrase is two abilities. | `crest:10544120` |
| give it +N/+N | On a when-enter ability "it" is pick:entering; on Obsessed Test Subject 10931110 the enter-route subject is pick:self (owner 2026-08-30 named_enter_count). | `10931110`, `10022210` |
